  <clipboard-copy aria-label="Copy link" for="issue-6255373419-permalink" role="menuitem" data-dd-action-name="pr.description.copy_link" data-view-component="true" class="dropdown-item btn-link">

</span>            <span data-view-component="true">
  <clipboard-copy role="menuitem" data-dd-action-name="pr.description.copy_markdown" value="## Summary

A case-folded loader suffix no longer borrows the lowercase suffix&#39;s module mode. `Fixes #8973`.

## Problem and expected behavior

`.MJS`/`.MTS` were classified as explicit ES modules and `.CJS`/`.CTS` as CommonJS because the suffix tests used case-insensitive regular expressions (`/\.m[jt]s$/iu`, `/\.cjs$/iu`), and the two display-side lookups lowercased the extension before comparing.

Node dispatches on the exact suffix, so a `main.MJS` entry in a `{&quot;type&quot;:&quot;commonjs&quot;}` package runs as CommonJS. REA emitted `module_format: &quot;esm&quot;` for it, which is false positive module-format evidence on a valid entrypoint.

Expected: a case-folded spelling must not by itself establish a loader. Preserve the path and inert syntax facts and retain `unknown` when no other observation establishes the loader.

## Change and scope

Loader mode is now derived from the exact extension in one place (`exactSuffix`/`isCommonJS`-style helpers in `javascriptSourceParser.ts`) and consumed by the parser runtime context, the semantic module links, and `moduleResolutionModeForJavaScriptReference`, which each lowercased the path before comparing.

Intentionally unchanged: case-folded extensions still admit the JavaScript grammar (`parserPluginsForPath`), and package-context rules still apply. Only the loader assertion changes.

Not applicable for the scope decision requirement: ordinary fix within existing boundaries.

## Contract and boundary impact

- Semantic owner and earliest changed stage: `javascriptSourceParser.ts` owns the parser runtime context; the same exact-suffix rule now also governs `JavaScriptModuleRelationships.moduleFormat` and `javascriptModuleResolutionMode`. Superseded case-insensitive suffix tests removed.
- Contribution scope fit and linked maintainer decision: not applicable.
- CLI and MCP/tool-catalog contract: none. `module_format` values keep their existing enum; only an incorrect value changes to `unknown`/`commonjs`.
- Provider, bridge, target-format, or platform compatibility: none.
- Evidence, artifact, provenance, or reconstruction contract: more accurate, no schema change.
- Target selection, authority, upstream behavior, and cleanup details: none.
- Generated metadata, package, or installation impact: none.

Expected effects (check known effects; use documentation-only on its own).
Unchecked items do not establish absence; leave uncertainties visible.

- [x] Read existing files or captured data.
- [ ] Launch or attach to a process.
- [ ] Inject instrumentation or run scripts/code.
- [ ] Change process memory, registers, files, or configuration.
- [ ] Contact network services.
- [ ] Recover protected code or work around protections.
- [ ] Documentation or usability only; no target interaction.
- [ ] Not sure yet; investigation or maintainer guidance needed.

## Evidence and regression coverage

- Tests added or updated: `src/domain/javascript/javascriptSourceParserContext.test.ts` gains five rows to the existing suffix\u2192context table covering `.MJS`, `.CJS`, `.MTS`, and `.D.CTS`, each asserting the context stays `unknown` rather than borrowing the lowercase suffix&#39;s mode.
- Base reproduction or other evidence: on the base revision, `/\.m[jt]s$/iu` matches `main.MJS` and `/\.cjs$/iu` matches `main.CJS`, so both were assigned the lowercase suffix&#39;s mode.
- User-visible CLI/MCP output (if applicable): the issue&#39;s fixture (a `{&quot;type&quot;:&quot;commonjs&quot;,&quot;main&quot;:&quot;main.MJS&quot;}` package with `main.MJS` requiring `dep.cjs`) now reports `module_format: &quot;commonjs&quot;` for `main.MJS` instead of `&quot;esm&quot;`.
- Remaining proof gaps: the grammar-admission and package-context paths are covered by the passing existing suite, but no new end-to-end acceptance test was added for the uppercase entrypoint; the CLI observation above is the end-to-end evidence.

For evidence-bearing changes:

- [x] Observed, derived, and inferred claims remain distinguishable.
- [x] Artifact identity, source provenance, and failed attempts remain preserved.
- [x] Unsupported, incomplete, unavailable, or uncertain outcomes remain visible.

## Validation performed

- `npx vitest run src/domain/javascript/javascriptSourceParserContext.test.ts` \u2014 27 passed
- `npx vitest run src/domain/javascript/ src/application/javascript/ src/composition/javascriptAnalysisWorkerProcess` \u2014 203 files, 5698 tests passed
- `npx vitest run tests/acceptance/applications/javascriptModuleResolutionSemantics.test.ts tests/acceptance/applications/unboundRequires.test.ts` \u2014 7 passed
- `npm run check:fast` \u2014 typecheck and lint both successful
- `npm run build:cached` \u2014 build successful
- `node scripts/rea.mjs analyze-javascript-application /tmp/fx8973 --json` (issue fixture) \u2014 exit 0, `main.MJS` reported `module_format: &quot;commonjs&quot;`

## Compatibility, safety, and release

- Breaking changes or migration steps: none. Previously incorrect uppercase-suffix loader values now read `unknown` or the package-declared mode, which is the accurate result.
- Real Hopper/Ghidra, browser, or OS coverage: not applicable; this is static source analysis. Verified on macOS arm64 with Node 24.22.2.
- Package or release metadata impact: none.
- Security, privacy, process, or containment review: none.

- [x] I checked the evidence above, confirmed it supports the current change, and recorded meaningful validation gaps.

        <code>
          <a title="fix(javascript): keep case-folded loader suffixes unknown

A case-insensitive suffix test gave `.MJS`/`.MTS` the same explicit
module mode as `.mjs`, and `.CJS`/`.CTS` the same CommonJS mode, even
though Node dispatches on the exact suffix. The stock runtime runs a
`main.MJS` entry as CommonJS, but the source-module row declared `esm`.

Classify loader mode from the exact extension in one place and use it
from the parser context, the semantic module links, and the resolution
mode lookup, which each lowercased the path before comparing. The
case-folded extensions still admit the JavaScript grammar and keep the
package-context rules; only the loader assertion changes to unknown." data-pjax="true" class="Link--secondary markdown-title" href="/morluto/rea/pull/9234/commits/86dfb90365d248dbb286117c9e675b50a8897011">fix(javascript): keep case-folded loader suffixes unknown</a>
        </code>

    <div class="Details-content--hidden mt-2">
      <pre class="color-fg-muted ws-pre-wrap">A case-insensitive suffix test gave `.MJS`/`.MTS` the same explicit
module mode as `.mjs`, and `.CJS`/`.CTS` the same CommonJS mode, even
though Node dispatches on the exact suffix. The stock runtime runs a
`main.MJS` entry as CommonJS, but the source-module row declared `esm`.

Classify loader mode from the exact extension in one place and use it
from the parser context, the semantic module links, and the resolution
mode lookup, which each lowercased the path before comparing. The
case-folded extensions still admit the JavaScript grammar and keep the
package-context rules; only the loader assertion changes to unknown.</pre>
    </div>

<h3 dir="auto">Summary</h3>
<p dir="auto">The PR stops uppercase loader suffixes from borrowing lowercase module modes.</p>

<li>JavaScript analysis no longer treats uppercase suffixes as loader proof.</li>

        <code>
          <a title="fix(javascript): keep the module grammar for .cts sources

Routing Babel&#39;s sourceType through isCommonJsSourcePath made .cts parse
as a script, so TypeScript import x = require() failed and every
relationship in the file was downgraded to partial.

Only .cjs asserts the CommonJS script wrapper; .cts keeps the module
grammar and takes its loader mode from the runtime context, which is
what the exact-suffix change was meant to alter." data-pjax="true" class="Link--secondary markdown-title" href="/morluto/rea/pull/9234/commits/1874f8729f7fbe4b1a5d42401921b1b2f2f08302">fix(javascript): keep the module grammar for .cts sources</a>
        </code>

          <span class="hidden-text-expander inline">
              <button aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Commit message body" type="button" data-view-component="true" class="ellipsis-expander js-details-target btn">    &hellip;
